**Starting-room reporting and duplicate-plan recovery verified (2026-09-15):** `mission-observation-v1` addresses a second, distinct real-model failure on the updated 8026 backend. Run `59abb68c-5c13-4d89-81bc-dd94b547ecbd`, session `a7acf55d-dd02-47a1-bb3c-45e57e92b50d`, accepted the short bathroom plan, then submitted two `observe_room` reports of the starting kitchen without a place ID. That action is reserved for verified named-room arrival. After those rejections Luna repeated the identical plan, hitting the third correction failure with `Mission plan is already established`. The run stopped at 8 simulated seconds after 18,047 input and 1,069 output tokens. This confirms the earlier target-length correction was exercised successfully in this run, but does not establish navigation success. [Preserved original sequence](../.runtime/mission-observation-v1/original-failure.json).

Hypothesis: separating observation reporting from arrival verification, exposing state-appropriate next actions and acknowledging an identical accepted plan avoids this protocol dead end without changing the user's task. New `report_observation` requires current fixture evidence, checks its 15-second decision age, records sensor/observation sequence and explicitly marks identity and arrival unverified. It adds no receipt, place label, map geometry or motion. The model receives `available_actions` derived from mission state and typed observed place entries: planning only before acceptance, and room-arrival reporting only after a target has been reached. Existing worker checks remain authoritative. Instructions distinguish starting-room reports from arrival, and require keeping the accepted plan. An identical repeated plan is acknowledged with next-action guidance without reconfiguration; a changed plan remains rejected. Original deadline, request limits, Home identity, Stop and receipt requirements stay intact.

The first seven focused checks and subsequent eight-case recovery gate pass. The final 24-case mission gate passes in 98.54 s, covering both a corrected starting-room report and the observed two-invalid-reports/identical-plan sequence followed by a report and actual head inspection. The accepted goal, Home identity and deadline remain unchanged; all scripted requests count toward the same budget, no arrival receipt appears, and the robot ends stopped. Adjacent target-length feedback, schema rejection, in-place turns, mid-turn Stop and stale-authority/receipt tests pass. [First checks](../.runtime/mission-observation-v1/first.xml), [recovery checks](../.runtime/mission-observation-v1/recovery.xml), [final regressions](../.runtime/mission-observation-v1/final.xml). This is scripted decisions with real kitchen/bathroom physics, not a new Luna evaluation or successful bathroom navigation.

**Mission target validation repair verified (2026-09-15):** `mission-validation-v1` addresses a reproduced prompt/schema mismatch in the kitchen/bathroom mission. The existing real-model run `cb96185f-41b3-4402-9d9a-e377db9c6bfe` returned three `plan.target` strings of 416, 317 and 349 characters against an unchanged 160-character limit. All three fail local replay solely on that field. The controller discarded field errors and provided only generic correction feedback, then ended without an accepted plan after 12,306 input and 1,070 output tokens. This is retained real-model failure evidence, not a new model evaluation. [Original responses and usage](../.runtime/mission-validation-v1/original-failure.json).

The schema and instructions now define `target` as a short identifying label rather than the full task; the original user goal remains available. Sanitized field-level validation details reach the next request, the trace and the final error. Text limits, three-attempt retry cap, inference budgets, motion authority and evidence checks are unchanged. Ten focused checks pass, including actual kitchen/bathroom preparation with scripted invalid/short-corrected plans and three-invalid-plan termination; rejected plans authorize no capability execution. [Schema checks](../.runtime/mission-validation-v1/schema.xml), [Correction-loop checks](../.runtime/mission-validation-v1/correction.xml). No new paid inference or proven bathroom arrival. Source SHA `7c5e75d9515dc4edd01f23a611b39da3823a246c3ccbd9af7517999e6e93666f` remained frozen; original map unchanged and no runtime mismatch. The live 8025 backend retains the original failed run and has not loaded the repair.

**Dead-end half-turn guidance verified with scripted physics (2026-09-15):** `mission-half-turn-v1` includes the validation repair and tests the hypothesis that explicit one-half-turn guidance lets Luna recover an observed dead end using the existing guarded `turn` capability. The instructions give a `turn_rad=3.14159` example, allow the opposite sign when evidence favors it, require reassessment of the new view and prohibit repeated spinning, inferred unseen free space, blind driving and clearance bypass. No wheel executor, sensor policy or safety threshold changed; local exploration stays independent of inference.

The final combined gate passes all 13 schema/correction/half-turn checks in 82.98 s. Requested plus/minus pi yielded 3.11936 / -3.12505 rad with 0.000377 / 0.000419 m displacement in real kitchen/bathroom physics. Every recorded turn buffer requested zero linear velocity, sampled contacts were empty, the following decision received a newer observation with the turned pose, and final state was stopped. Mid-turn Stop interrupted at 0.32556 rad with 0.000057 m displacement and no next model request. The first run retained two passes and a test-only failure from awaiting the synchronous Stop API. The subsequent three-pass run correctly measured the turns but its final scripted wait duration was below the schema minimum; the final gate corrects that duration to 0.5 s and asserts zero rejected decisions. Neither test repair changed production code. [Initial checks](../.runtime/mission-half-turn-v1/first.xml), [intermediate metrics](../.runtime/mission-half-turn-v1/verified.xml), [final checks and metrics](../.runtime/mission-half-turn-v1/final.xml). This exercises the existing kitchen/bathroom start, not every possible wedged pose or contact condition. Real-Luna recovery selection, repeated-spin avoidance and task success remain unmeasured.

The complete fixed household series against unchanged `robot-power-v1-20260915` also retains 18 passes, zero failures and three blocked room prerequisites, with no invalid or unrun cases. Suite, fixture, map, scoring and runtime settings match; source SHA `723adf48aadd648bb37c07c9412df478463815140631b98d33a3be620ca7895f` stayed frozen, all 102 measured source/build hashes matched after the final test-only correction, and the original map is unchanged. This scripted executive comparison is a regression check, not evidence of Luna recovery choice or general navigation improvement. **Unified robot controls and clearer cockpit (2026-09-15):** `unified-controls-v1` tests the UI hypothesis that one persistent command bar and one primary robot status reduce ambiguity without changing the underlying control APIs. Power, Start and Stop now share that bar; Reset and manual enable/takeover are in its options menu. The existing mission form submits through its native form association. Camera, spatial-map and challenge dialogs receive the same command bar through a portal, with no extra Stop button; closing a dialog restores it to the cockpit. Local navigation and Home tasks use global Stop. The mission panel prioritizes the goal with larger 14 px form text, retains expandable connection/settings and trace views, and owns token/request accounting once. Sensor-health readouts remain distinct from robot execution status.

Activity and outcome are now resolved into one status, preserving the distinction between physics-verified success, an unverified agent report, blocked actions, power-off and lost connection. A retained interruption cannot override a resumed On/idle state. The initial focused browser run found that precedence defect (2 passes / 1 failure); its repair passed all three checks. A later 13-case final gate retained 12 passes and one test-migration failure: the test toggled an already-open options menu closed before Reset. Idempotent menu opening fixed it, and the isolated manual/Reset recheck passed. No application defect or safety threshold was changed for that test repair. [Initial result](../.runtime/unified-controls-v1/first.xml), [status repair](../.runtime/unified-controls-v1/status-recheck.xml), [final gate](../.runtime/unified-controls-v1/final.xml), [manual recheck](../.runtime/unified-controls-v1/manual-recheck.xml).

The production build passes. Focused evidence covers exactly one Start/Stop/status, native mission submission, explicit power cycles, modal Stop, manual enable/Reset, responsive sticky hit testing, nonblank interactive 3D and camera/map transitions at 1440/1024/390/320 px, preserved token totals and stale readings, and independent outcome evidence. All six existing Home-map browser workflows pass; the four accounting/status/local-only checks also pass. [Home workflows](../.runtime/unified-controls-v1/home-controls.xml), [accounting/status](../.runtime/unified-controls-v1/accounting-status.xml), [desktop](../.runtime/unified-controls-v1/controls-1440.png), [mobile](../.runtime/unified-controls-v1/controls-390.png), [320 px](../.runtime/unified-controls-v1/controls-320.png). These are mocked-transport or real-physics/scripted-fixture browser checks, not real-model evidence or a usability study. Full suites and navigation benchmarks were not rerun for this frontend-only change; backend control, freshness, saved maps and model inputs are unchanged. Earlier navigation failures remain unresolved.

The shared browser was moved from the inactive old 8024 server to `http://127.0.0.1:8025/` and refreshed to the final build. It exposes exactly one control bar/Start/Stop, at zero simulated seconds with an inactive agent and zero reported tokens. No real Luna requests, new server, cloud changes or scene reset were needed; existing backend processes were left running. Production source diagnostics are clean. No measured navigation, performance, billing or general-usability improvement is claimed.

**Robot power and bounded Luna usage candidate (2026-09-15):** `robot-power-v1` tests the hypothesis that explicit Off/On-idle/Working states, local idle throttling, no camera-triggered inference and bounded model usage reduce unnecessary processing while preserving motion authority. Power-off immediately interrupts the controller, revokes motion through the existing Stop revision, drains/cancels work, invalidates pending spatial jobs, suspends capture and unloads resident SmolVLA. Power-on preserves the scene/maps and starts no old task. A newer Stop during power-on prevents pending resume; Off survives episode reset. Startup remains On/idle for compatibility, and power state is not persisted. Stop remains independent of power.

Idle RGB/depth and home sensor submissions are limited to a 2 s interval, preview submissions use the idle interval when requested, WebSocket state publication slows from 50 ms to 1 s, and map polling uses 2 s idle / 5 s Off intervals. The map labels deliberate reduced-duty operation Idle while retaining real sensor errors and stale guards. No freshness/clearance thresholds are relaxed; active navigation still requires fresh footprint evidence. This is reduced submission cadence, not a measured CPU/GPU reduction or complete suspension of all UI rendering. Legacy text and voice camera-triggered wake-ups are removed; explicit voice input remains available without automatic recording.

Every text model call now passes through a controller budget adapter; voice responses use equivalent gates. Request counts increment before submission, including failed/cancelled attempts. Browser defaults are 12 requests / 100,000 reported input+output tokens; API defaults are 30 / 100,000. Request/token settings persist, and mid-task redirects retain counters and the original limits. Unknown response usage ends the task. Token thresholds are checked after usage is returned and may overshoot by one response; cancelling an in-flight request does not guarantee zero provider billing, and usage for such a request may remain unknown. This is not an exact monetary cap. After an approved plain-exploration plan, bounded local exploration continues without repeated model checkpoints; semantic object/room paths remain model-guided. The existing first-plan dependency, timeout/403 handling, and active sensor latency are not fixed.

Completed evidence is **scripted/unit/API/browser**, not real-model validation: four initial idle tests pass; request/token gates pass; three edge tests prove 0 idle versus 1 active capture submission at a 0.3 s sample age, no forced capture while Off, Stop dominance during power-on and retained redirect budgets. A real-physics park exploration diagnostic completes a local capability after exactly one scripted plan request. All 15 then-current voice tests plus power/API/exploration checks passed (18 total); two additional voice-budget checks and the no-camera-wake test pass (3 total). [Idle tests](../.runtime/robot-power-v1/no-idle-inference.xml), [budget tests](../.runtime/robot-power-v1/budgets-fixed.xml), [edge tests](../.runtime/robot-power-v1/edge-cases-fixed.xml), [single-request exploration](../.runtime/robot-power-v1/local-continuation.xml), [voice and power](../.runtime/robot-power-v1/voice-and-power.xml), [voice budgets](../.runtime/robot-power-v1/voice-budget.xml), [final seven guards](../.runtime/robot-power-v1/final-guards.xml).

The broader controller/API/preferences run retained **169 passes and 7 failures**: five obsolete sleeping/auto-wake expectations were updated for the requested behavior; two local-navigation scan/start failures passed on isolation, without controller tuning. The targeted recheck passed all 17 cases, but the entire 176-test batch has not been rerun, so no full-green claim is made. One initial budget test used the wrong outcome field, and one added-test patch had a collection error; both were corrected and passing reruns retained. The initial power browser test found overlapping fixed toolbar controls; moving power into the existing toolbar resolved it. Eight final power/mission/HUD workflows pass (60.0 s) before the final narrow reset-race and 320 px brand-spacing adjustments; the production build and final seven backend guards pass afterward. [Broader run](../.runtime/robot-power-v1/backend.xml), [API recheck](../.runtime/robot-power-v1/api-recheck.xml), [initial browser](../.runtime/robot-power-v1/browser.xml), [browser repair](../.runtime/robot-power-v1/browser-recheck.xml), [eight browser workflows](../.runtime/robot-power-v1/browser-final.xml), [desktop UI](../.runtime/robot-power-v1/ui-1440.png), [mobile UI](../.runtime/robot-power-v1/ui-390.png). After the final reset guard and 320 px spacing adjustment, the final-build power/budget and responsive interactive 3D workflows pass **2/2 in 33.2 s**, including refreshed desktop/mobile screenshots, nonblank canvas checks and layout checks. **Image-only micro HUD and independent map telemetry (2026-09-15):** `viewport-micro-hud-v2` supersedes the header-only minimization below. Hypothesis: keeping the same live camera/map at 96 px wide on desktop and 72 px on mobile provides useful peripheral views without text or header bars. Each miniature is a full-area, keyboard-accessible restore button; headers, metadata, expand icons and map label text disappear only in the miniature. The original image/map continues updating, and expanded dialogs remain available after restoring. No sensing is automatically enabled, and no robot control, model input, saved map or backend safety policy changes.

Two mocked-transport regressions reproduced a frontend defect: RGB/depth image expiry made valid map telemetry Unavailable, and stalled image loads blocked map publication. Map polling now publishes independently every 750 ms after each telemetry response, while one bounded paired-image load runs separately; image, command and sensor errors are distinct. A canvas-ref handoff fix preserves drawing when closing the map dialog. Visual inspection also found that a global button-hover rule covered the miniature with an opaque background; the final test checks the composed overlay alpha, not just pixels underneath it. Earlier [focused](../.runtime/viewport-micro-hud-v2/focused.xml) and [canvas](../.runtime/viewport-micro-hud-v2/final.xml) reports lacked that hover assertion and do not establish visible-thumbnail correctness.

The production build and **five focused browser workflows pass, 46.8 s**: two mocked image-failure tests plus real-physics/scripted-fixture HUD, expanded-camera Stop and paired RGB/depth workflows. At 1440, 1024, 390 and 320 px, assertions cover live image updates, independent miniature/restore state, zero visible miniature text, fixed aspect ratios, nonblank map after dialog/miniature transitions, translucent hover overlays, nonblank interactive 3D, Escape/focus restoration and no overflow/overlap. [Verified tests](../.runtime/viewport-micro-hud-v2/verified.xml), [desktop miniature](../.runtime/viewport-micro-hud-v2/minimized-1440.png), [320 px miniature](../.runtime/viewport-micro-hud-v2/minimized-320.png), [normal desktop](../.runtime/viewport-micro-hud-v2/hud-1440.png), [normal mobile](../.runtime/viewport-micro-hud-v2/hud-390.png). Existing test-file Node/VisualProbe editor diagnostics remain; production source diagnostics are clean. Full suites and navigation benchmarks were not rerun for this frontend-only change; no general performance or navigation gain is measured.

Separately, read-only operator diagnostics on port 8000 confirmed genuine backend aging: frame 4084 aged from 0.906 to 1.172 s across four requests, crossing the unchanged 1 s stale threshold. Cumulative counters at that point were 3,471 accepted results, 563 expired results, maximum capture 0.813 s and maximum post-capture processing 1.500 s. These are existing-run diagnostic counters, not a matched benchmark or causal attribution. The shared 8024 preview was stopped at 0 simulated seconds with sensing disabled; it was not enabled or moved. Backend freshness delays and the earlier model timeout/403 failures remain separate unresolved issues. Real-model behavior and persistence of miniature preferences are not measured; miniature state remains mount-local.

**Camera and minimizable spatial-map HUD implemented (2026-09-15):** `viewport-sensor-hud-v1` tests the usability hypothesis that the head camera and observed local map can remain visible beside the 3D scene without opening separate panels. Both are now anchored inside the upper-right of the world viewport, with independent minimize/restore headers and expanded dialogs. The camera's existing feed and the spatial component's single canvas/poller are reused; minimizing keeps them mounted and does not stop sensing or motion. Sensing remains opt-in, its controls remain below the viewport, and the HUD labels Off/Waiting/Live/Stale/Paused/Disconnected/Unavailable states. The map remains the sensor-observed 8 x 8 m wheel-odometry view, not hidden spectator geometry or the saved whole-home map. Proprioception stays below the scene; the graphics selector moves lower-left to avoid the HUD.

The production build and **three focused Playwright workflows pass** (34.7 s): HUD layout/independent minimization, expanded live camera/Stop, and paired RGB/depth/unknown-map preservation. Actual screenshots and pixel checks at 1440, 1024, 390 and 320 px verify nonblank 3D/map rendering, right anchoring, no page overflow, no widget overlap, compact headers at most 40 px tall, dialog Escape/focus restoration, orbit interaction and camera updates while minimized. No model-service calls or controller changes in this UI slice. **Mid-motion task-change regression added; adaptation remains blocked (2026-09-15):** `unified-task-change-v1` tests the hypothesis that an operator can replace an active cabinet approach with a saved-Home task while retaining the original episode and remaining budget. The enhanced-physics scripted scenario prepares an isolated sensor-built Home, surveys the surrounding floor, starts the cabinet approach, then sends the replacement through `AgentController.redirect` after at least 0.30 m displacement. It checks old-mission cancellation, old-session/result rejection, changed goal delivery, unchanged deadline/remaining turns, Home completion and stopped final position within 0.15 m. No model service is called and no evaluator geometry is supplied to the scripted responder. [Scenario source](../tests/test_mission.py).

The test exposed a missing startup resume in the unified runner: after redirect's Stop, its initial scan was rejected as cancelled. Startup now uses the existing `resume_manual(expected_stop_revision=...)` guard and a live-session check. The focused final result is **one passed, one failed**: a newer Stop prevents pending restart/scanning; the cabinet-to-Home test reaches redirect, passes cancellation/budget/stale-session checks, delivers the replacement goal, but cannot complete because Home verification rejects `Stopping footprint lacks observed parking margin; scan the surrounding floor`. An initial floor survey did not resolve this. The failure remains in the suite; this is not a demonstrated successful adaptation or real-Luna result. [Focused results](../.runtime/unified-mission-v1/task-change-focused.xml), [pre-resume failure](../.runtime/unified-mission-v1/task-change-setup.xml), [rejection diagnostic](../.runtime/unified-mission-v1/task-change-diagnostic.xml), [first setup attempt](../.runtime/unified-mission-v1/task-change-first.xml).

Only the unified startup guard and tests changed in this slice; safety, clearance, freshness, lease, motion and scorer thresholds are unchanged. The added survey's turn allowance is fixture setup, not a larger budget granted by redirect; the original 150-second deadline is preserved. Return-clearance diagnosis and a new complete fixed-series qualification are pending. These runtime edits postdate frozen `unified-map-mission-v1` source SHA `8df4197d07684291a2a6f5bac385bfe6f2921cf2a48e6b6bea9ca265c66b3187`; earlier benchmark results do not qualify this revision. Original-start preservation without a named Home and real-model instruction understanding remain untested.

**Guided safety changes tested; two-room survey remains blocked (2026-09-15):** `guided-waypoints-v1` / mapped controller `0.4.0` replaces raw map-panel translation with footprint-planned `guided_to` and adds fresh-sensor/whole-robot guards to mapping-mode raw drive. The initial operator-guided draft reached121.70m2/14,083known cells with one tentative doorway, then had two contact episodes and localization loss. Later planned enhanced/CPU attempts failed on buffer expiry/stale depth. [108 final focused checks](../.runtime/room-guided-20260915/final-regressions.xml), build and final responsive browser test pass, but no Kitchen image, reviewed room map or physical round trip is qualified. Original v1 inputs and failed recordings preserved; no new Luna inference. [Full evidence and next isolation gate](../.runtime/guided-waypoints-review/summary.md).

**Sensing diagnosis completed without a demonstrated survey gain:** raw capture now overlaps one pending map job without replacing it; batched depth and integer-cell lidar deduplication match the old evidence arrays. Instrumented exact surveys before/after capture overlap travelled3.220m/2.120m and added11.11m2/8.74m2, both retry-exhausted. Later batched-depth/cell-ID diagnostics also failed; differing source/harness/load prevents a causal comparison. Nineteen simulator listeners and substantial editor/browser/WSL activity were observed but left untouched; contention is a hypothesis, not established cause. All failure artifacts remain linked in the [review](../.runtime/guided-waypoints-review/summary.md). No freshness, lease or speed limits were relaxed.

**Room-memory software implemented; Kitchen/report/Home milestone remains unmet:** `room-memory-v1` / mapped controller `0.3.0` adds grounded topology/frontiers, image-backed tentative labels, operator review and a single-budget room/report/return executive. The 74-check map/recording/benchmark gate, final build and responsive review test pass. Two recorded surveys failed on stale depth before 0.28 m; the room-v2 baseline passes unknown-ID rejection but retains two blocked room prerequisites. One frozen real-Luna attempt timed out at 180.813 s / 8.127 m, with no Kitchen arrival, room observation or return; 1,796 complete samples, zero drops/contacts. It chose ordinary local actions, never the new saved-home capabilities. No semantic accuracy or task gain is established. Original v1 map/suite unchanged. [Evidence, failures and remaining work](../.runtime/room-memory-review/summary.md).

**Mapped-motion refresh demonstrates a matched fixed-suite gain:** `mapped-motion-refresh-v1` / mapped controller `0.2.0` passed checkpoint navigation, checkpoint-return, useful exploration and actual mid-motion Stop 3/3 each, versus 0/3 in the frozen original baseline. Localization and unsafe-destination rejection remain3/3; all three room-to-room prerequisites remain blocked. Same suite, map, fixtures and budgets; all92candidate source/build files unchanged,18complete recordings/5295samples/zero drops/zero sampled contacts. Median checkpoint8.422s and roundtrip24.453s; exploration+5.34..7.40m2netfree within30s.42safety/workflow and30neighboring checks pass. No Luna, whole-house or general-reliability claim. [Comparison and limits](../.runtime/mapped-motion-refresh-review/summary.md).

**Standardized household baseline completed:** `household-foundation-v1` now has seven fixed tasks, three starting offsets and 21 retained outcomes. Hinted localization and occupied-destination safe rejection passed 3/3 each; mapped navigation, Home return, useful exploration and reaching the mid-motion Stop injection point passed 0/3 each. Room-to-room had three blocked prerequisites because the frozen partial map has no two room annotations. All 18 physical attempts have complete recordings, 3,115 samples, zero drops and zero sampled contacts; all 91 source/build files and the original map stayed unchanged. This is a negative deterministic-control baseline, not Luna autonomy or an improvement claim. [Protocol](HOUSEHOLD_BENCHMARK.md), [results](#standardized-household-baseline).

**Spatial progress capture and replay is being verified:** `spatial-replay-v1` extends the common recorder with immutable observed-map snapshots, visit masks, compact mission/sensor telemetry and bounded event history. Operator workflows automatically archive with separate evidence labels; the Test Archive adds synchronized camera/depth/map replay, coverage history and per-attempt spatial outcomes. A scripted real-physics artifact retains a controller-confirmed Home return and budget-limited expansion on an isolated copy of the saved map; no Luna calls, benchmark gain or saved-map mutation. [Design and limitations](SPATIAL_PROGRESS.md).

**Persistent home mapping implemented as an initial simulator workflow:** `persistent-home-map-v1` adds sensor-built saved geometry, named room/doorway/destination links, scan localization, a worker-owned mission executive, frontier expansion and separate image-backed object observations. Eighteen focused tests and the final mapping UI test pass; the build passes. A live operator-guided partial shared-apartment map was saved once and reused unchanged after a scenario change, then localized from a fresh scan and a named-place hint. The 12-second scripted expansion probe moved 0.226 m, added 160 known cells and ended at its budget, not complete coverage. Broader gates retain non-mapping TinyRenderer stale-depth and browser timing/menu failures; no full-green suite or real-model improvement claim. [Evidence](#persistent-home-map), [workflow and limitations](HOME_MAPPING.md).

**Nav2 real-model comparison blocked:** `nav2-task-pilot-v1` planned six runs with matched task hashes, Luna/high, enhanced rendering, two images, 8,192 context tokens, adaptive/history enabled, composer/handoff disabled and a 180-second time-only budget. [Preflight](../.runtime/performance/nav2-task-pilot-preflight-20260914/experiment.json) passed without inference. The [measured attempt](../.runtime/performance/nav2-task-pilot-v1-20260914/experiment.json) reached only Built-in parking: Foundry HTTP 403, zero input/output tokens and no model response. Its recording is complete/zero-drop/zero-contact, but it is not a task-capability result. `backend/worker.py` changed externally during the attempt; the source guard cancelled the remaining five cases. No inference retry or access-policy bypass, no matched performance claim.

**Nav2 follow-up did not demonstrate improvement:** the [eight scripted local probes](../.runtime/performance/nav2-local-pilot-v1-20260914/experiment.json) produced three Built-in arrivals and zero Nav2 arrivals, but source drift and pre-scan readiness checks invalidate a clean comparison. Nav2 had one command watchdog and three rejected goals; Built-in had one stale-depth stop. All recordings are complete, 373 samples/zero drops/zero sampled contacts. [Outcome table and limitations](NAV2.md#comparison-attempt-2026-09-14). No performance gain or causal regression is claimed. The evaluation-only runner now waits for a fresh post-scan ready heartbeat and fails final-source drift. A [no-inference preflight](../.runtime/performance/nav2-postscan-preflight-20260914/experiment.json) reached readiness but was again invalidated by source changes; no new motion results replaced the failed probes. Further trials stopped pending model access and a stable source snapshot.

**Nav2 primary local integration verified; built-in backup retained:** the final frozen-source real Jazzy check reached two sequential operator-selected local goals and passed mid-motion Stop, frozen physics and old-command rejection after Stop/resume. No model inference or full parking mission was tested. The 38-check scoped gate and final four-case API contract gate pass; the production build and primary/backup selector test pass. Full suites are not green: non-Nav2 backend and archive failures remain documented below. Nav2 replaces bounded destination planning/tracking, not semantic candidates or marked-floor approach. No SLAM loop closure, automatic fallback, performance gain or full-task qualification is claimed. [Scope and startup](NAV2.md), [evidence and failures](#nav2-primary-integration).

**Control-recovery milestone complete; kitchen capability remains unqualified:** continuous/composer 0.5.0 brakes for stopped review, recovers ordinary motion-lease expiry within three attempts and separates optional prose from executable validation. Stop and task deadlines remain terminal; safety/timing thresholds are unchanged. The final focused gate passes 193 tests; nine authority/recorded-inspection and seven milestone checks pass. Two frozen real-model kitchen diagnostics scored 0/2: one timeout at 180.641 s / 9.262 m, one unknown/obstructed path failure at 26.578 s / 0.510 m. One 547-character reason was normalized and the valid action continued without a schema retry. Neither trial exercised lease recovery, so that remains scripted/recorded-physics evidence. Both recordings are complete/unassisted/zero-drop/zero sampled contacts, no false completion claims; all 84 measured files stayed unchanged. No task-success improvement is established. [Results and limitations](../.runtime/performance/control-recovery-v1-20260914-review/summary.md).

**Motion skills v4 complete: one real no-feasible recovery, no task-success gain.** The new structured rejection was exercised once (35.959 s estimated / 8.750 s lease remaining), followed by fresh unchanged-pose feedback and a successful scan. No raw sub-minimum cruise/curve request occurred in the pilot. Baseline 0/2, candidate 0/2 verified successes; zero objectives, all recordings complete/unassisted/zero-drop/zero sampled contacts and no unverified completion claims. All three composition proposals were rejected before motion: one on timing, two on geometry. Both candidates timed out; one baseline exhausted schema corrections. The specific sizing-feedback defect is addressed, but useful composition and search/target verification remain unqualified. Composer 0.4.0 stays default-off. Four option/version checks, 12 enhanced worker/controller checks and 179 focused tiny regressions pass; 84 measured source/build files stayed unchanged. [Results and limits](../.runtime/performance/motion-skills-v4-20260914-review/summary.md).

**Motion skills v3 pilot complete: timing admission rejects, useful composition remains unproven.** Baseline 0/2 and composer 0/2 verified task successes, zero objectives. Both proposed approach/inspect plans were rejected before motion (29.61 s estimated / 8.44 s remaining; 23.57 / 11.83). Neither composition executed. One candidate exhausted the session budget; the other exhausted its bounded schema corrections after requesting cruise lengths below the legal minimum. Returned length ceilings were themselves below every legal moving component, so explicit no-feasible-plan handling remains a gap. Both baselines made unverified completion claims. Composer 0.3.0 stays default-off; 84 source/build files stayed unchanged and all four recordings are complete/unassisted/zero-drop/zero sampled contacts. Five slow-clock/endpoint checks, four enhanced controller/Stop checks and 175 focused tiny regressions pass; no full-suite or task-competence claim. [Results and limitations](../.runtime/performance/motion-skills-v3-20260914-review/summary.md).

**Motion skills v2 pilot complete: schema recovery worked, full-task success did not improve.** Baseline 0/2, composer 0/2, zero independently completed objectives. One candidate corrected two invalid responses (wrong action/plan combination and reason 531 > 500 characters) using fresh feedback within unchanged budgets. Its later 1.480 m approach passed admission at 7.752 s estimated / 10.594 s remaining, yet timed out after 10.797 s with 0.046 m remaining. No real-model budget rejection or inspection was observed; the estimator remains insufficient for reliable admission. All four recordings are complete/unassisted/zero-drop/zero sampled contacts, 84 source/build files unchanged. Keep composer 0.2.0 default-off; continuous is 0.4.0. Software evidence: five correction/deadline, five pause-authority and 15 lease/physics checks pass; enhanced regression 165 passed/four legacy failures, those four pass tiny. No full-green enhanced or capability claim. [Results, traces and limitations](../.runtime/performance/motion-skills-v2-20260914-review/summary.md).

**Motion skill composer pilot complete; keep default off:** two frozen real-model trials per setting yielded **baseline 0/2 and composer 0/2 verified task successes**. One composed approach/inspection completed locally; a second exhausted its observation-relative lease after a 14.328 s model reply. The other candidate ended on a 559-character reason exceeding the 500-character schema limit; baseline made one unverified completion claim. All four recordings are complete/unassisted/zero-drop/zero sampled contacts, and 84 source/build files stayed unchanged. Build, 157 focused backend checks and the full browser gate (48 passed, one skipped) pass; they do not establish task competence. Prioritize bounded schema recovery and lease-aware plan sizing before cross-plan blending. [Design](#motion-skill-composer), [pilot results and limitations](../.runtime/performance/motion-skills-20260914-review/summary.md).

**Live six-capability sweep cancelled after four attempted stages:** the table/clockwise circuit independently passed at 78.328 s; object search exhausted its 180 s budget without completing inspection. Parking was invalidated by a recording PermissionError, and kitchen search stopped on operator disconnect at 132.266 s. Crossing and recharge did not run. All four retained recordings have zero sampled contacts/drops; this mixed interrupted sweep is not a success-rate estimate. Browser configuration differs from prior CLI trials, so no matched performance claim is made. Robot verified inactive; no automatic restart or retry. [Results and limitations](#live-common-capability-sweep).

**Sensor-dwell 0.3.0 remains experimental after two unchanged real-model recharge trials:** neither completed the full task or selected `wait_until`/`advance_subgoal`. The first completed survey and return before timeout at 21.692% charge. The newly requested repeat completed only survey, then stopped at 118.734 s because its sixth response had a 552-character `reason` against the local 500-character limit. The model recognized the scan battery drop but kept seeking survey containment; no return occurred. Both recordings are complete/unassisted/zero-drop/zero-contact, with no false terminal completion claim. Prior 36 scoped checks do not establish model competence. Prioritize tool-contract correction and consistent subgoal/target state; do not extend budgets or weaken safety. [Repeat and capability gaps](../.runtime/performance/sensor-dwell-repeat-20260914-recharge/review.md), [first probe](../.runtime/performance/sensor-dwell-20260914-review/summary.md).

**Subgoal-progress 0.2.0 candidate:** one frozen real-Luna recharge trial escaped the survey loop, returned to the original charger and began charging. Independent objectives advanced from survey-only in the baseline to survey plus return; the full task still timed out, with last observed battery 54.044% rather than the required 90%. Recording complete, unassisted, zero-drop, zero sampled contacts and no false completion claim. This is partial progress in one matched task, not a reliability or causal gain. [Comparison and remaining dwell bottleneck](../.runtime/performance/subgoal-progress-20260914-review/summary.md).

**Current five-challenge baseline completed:** standalone parking passed at 95.500 s and the table/clockwise lap at 59.156 s. Shared-apartment object search and recharge timed out; kitchen search ended on a rejected path. All five recordings are complete, unassisted, zero-drop, zero sampled contacts and without false terminal completion claims. Recharge completed its survey but the controller/model remained stuck on repeated intermediate-area verification. Prioritize sensor-grounded subgoal progress and bounded inspection recovery; do not infer a general success rate or improvement from one attempt per task. [Full baseline and failure review](../.runtime/performance/cross-challenge-baseline-20260914-review/summary.md).

**Integrated model-test workspace:** cockpit/archive navigation now preserves the active control session. Browser trials appear while running and finalize automatically, with architecture versions, implementation fingerprints and separate model/configuration revisions. Real-model evidence is the archive default. Optional dotted scenario reference paths remain operator-only. This is workflow/provenance infrastructure, not a measured robot-performance improvement. [Versioning, verification and limitations](#integrated-model-test-workspace).

**Generic AI task control: unavailable in the current backend contract.** The historical experimental mode and its nine real-model development attempts are preserved below; those attempts did not establish the three example capabilities. The current on-disk AgentStart/controller does not expose the generic-route integration. The UI reports this capability as unavailable and ordinary Start omits the unsupported field. No historical controller implementation was restored for the test-workspace change. In the latest historical measured version the table trial moved 2.129 m but completed only 15/360 degrees before timeout; figure-eight and following reported limitations without useful motion. Reliable person tracking and figure-eight task verification remain unqualified. [Historical evidence and open requirements](#generic-ai-routes).

**Rolling exploration experiment:** `rolling-exploration-v1` now keeps moving during serialized Luna review by renewing short sensor-validated paths. Five recorded scripted safety/continuity cases passed. Two frozen real-model trials confirmed rolling motion but **neither completed its task**. Kitchen search used 27 local path renewals and 11 accepted supervisor reviews: stationary share 9.8% versus 43.9%, moving speed 0.330 versus 0.188 m/s in the preceding single trial. It travelled 19.035 m and timed out. Object search stopped at a blocked-path check after 67.3 s. Both recordings are complete/unassisted/zero-contact, with no false completion claim. This is descriptive continuity evidence, not a task-success, causal or reliability gain. [Comparison](../.runtime/performance/rolling-exploration-comparison-20260914/summary.md).

**Other-scenario pilot:** the 2026-09-14 frozen-code exploratory trials produced **no verified passes**: standalone parking was rejected before driving, shared-apartment object search and recharge timed out, and kitchen navigation made a false completion claim. Recharge completed only the survey objective. All four recordings are complete, unassisted and have zero sampled contacts. The circuit result does not establish general navigation competence. [Per-scenario results and evidence](#navigation-breadth-pilot).

**Rolling circuit experiment:** `rolling-circuit-v1` passed all five frozen-code real-Luna standalone table/clockwise trials, unassisted with complete recordings and zero sampled contacts. Median completion was **59.218 s versus 100.219 s** in the historical five-run baseline (40.9% shorter observed); median moving speed was **0.387 versus 0.184 m/s**. The 0.5 m/s command cap and safety limits are unchanged. Stationary wall time changed little (23.030 versus 24.391 s), so the main measured difference is sustained translation, not eliminating stationary waits. This is fixed-task development evidence with workload/intervening-source confounds, not a causal or general reliability estimate. [Comparison](../.runtime/performance/rolling-circuit-comparison-20260914/summary.md).

**The first matched pilot did not show an overall improvement from motion-camera history.** In eight real-Luna runs, current-only passed 2/4 selected challenges and history passed 1/4. Both passed Apartment Search, but history was slower (121.282 versus 101.031 seconds to verified completion); only current-only completed the furniture lap. History used 361,660 reported tokens versus 224,745, about 60.9% more across these four tasks. All recordings were complete with zero dropped records, zero recorded contacts and zero false terminal completion claims. This is one pair per challenge on a shared workstation, not a reliability or causal estimate. [Pilot report and artifacts](../.runtime/performance/history-ab-20260913/summary.md).

Brief-object recognition accuracy remains **not measured**. The furniture history run correctly identified the table, then encountered stale depth and recovery/path blocks. The results do not establish that history caused poorer recognition. Default history-input behavior remains unchanged. The bounded circuit freshness recovery below has scripted physics evidence, not a new real-model reliability estimate.

The 132.547-second successful furniture lap occurred **before** visual history. It establishes one table/clockwise capability result for the directed-scan controller, not a visual-history gain. Scenario preview pictures improve operator setup; they are never model inputs and establish no robot-performance gain.

## Baseline Archive And Local Exploration

Hypotheses: explicit map reuse prevents unwanted remapping, comparison cohorts prevent incompatible results from appearing as progress, and an inference-independent frontier executive can explore without spending Luna tokens on low-level steering. Designs `optional-map-reuse-v1`, `baseline-progress-v1`, `sensor-frontier-exploration-v1`. Affected workflows: scenario load/reset, shared/standalone saved-map exploration and archive/evaluator reporting. Existing safety thresholds, benchmark suite/map/scoring, and model prompts remain unchanged.

`ChallengeLoad.reuse_saved_map` defaults true; false skips loading without deleting the saved environment map. Reset retains the policy. Recorded map provenance includes mode/UUID/revision/hash/localization; map reuse still requires fresh localization. The explicit local mode creates an unsaved draft when no map is selected and uses the existing geometric frontier executive with zero model calls. Compatible bounded route continuations preserve velocity and original leases/deadlines, with fresh footprint/sensor/revocation checks; invalid continuation leaves the stopping endpoint intact. Bounded retries and timeouts remain, so this is not an always-moving or complete-coverage guarantee.

Archive Progress separates matching test cohorts, persists the operator's selected baseline and displays capability deltas, failed/blocked/invalid/not-run counts, identical-success timing pairs and historical task matrices. Drill-down selects a non-passing trial. Unmatched/incomplete metadata and operator diagnostics stay inspectable outside comparisons. Full household runs now require baseline lineage or explicit new-series creation and publish comparisons automatically. They do not rebuild the frozen input map or alter any scoring thresholds.

[Full candidate](../.runtime/performance/household-foundation-v1-progress-20260915/experiment.json): all 94 measured files unchanged, source SHA `bc42a60ab47b84d990ec90d3cf99adfa51fa52f4adc1dca25040c2eb3b9dca15`. The 21 outcomes are localization 3/3, navigation 2/3, return 1/3, occupied rejection 3/3, exploration 2/3, Stop 2/3 and three blocked room prerequisites. All 18 physical recordings are complete: 4,461 samples, zero drops, sampled contacts or manual relocations. Current pass counts exceed the original negative baseline but regress four tasks against the earlier motion-refresh run. One checkpoint arrival's independent dwell was 0.45 s against 0.5 s. Stop-r3 acknowledgement took 0.593 s against 0.5 s, although subsequent frozen physics, late-command rejection and no-motion Resume checks passed. Return failures were route retries and stale depth; explore-r3 travelled 0.0207 m with -0.05 m2 net free-area change. Failures remain recorded; no selected retries or causal gain claim. [Audit and both comparisons](../.runtime/baseline-progress-review/analysis.json).

[Separate no-model diagnostic](../.runtime/performance/sensor-frontier-exploration-v1-20260915/survey/report.json): 1.175 m travel, +5.71 m2 free area, two rolling continuations, two reached frontiers and one retry. The 30-second budget ended limited after 30.844 s including terminal processing. The recording has 325 complete samples and 18 map snapshots with zero drops, sampled contacts, manual relocations or model tokens. The original saved map is unchanged. This local mode is distinct from ordinary `explore` exercised by the full v1 suite; the diagnostic does not substitute for that series or prove policy superiority or full-house autonomy.

Verification: [89 focused backend checks](../.runtime/baseline-exploration-20260915-tests.xml), seven final contract checks, production build and six focused browser workflows pass. Live headless checks at 1440 and 390 px show seven tasks, four regressions against motion refresh, and no overflow, page errors or submitted robot commands; map and local-mode controls are visible. [Screenshots and report](../.runtime/baseline-progress-review/summary.md), [UI checks](../.runtime/baseline-progress-review/ui-checks.json). The shared browser's 3D route initially timed out; retry loaded 192 samples without affecting robot state. Full suites were not rerun. The user's port-8019 run is untouched; the new port-8020 preview uses an isolated original-map copy.

Upstream research confirms SLAM Toolbox's serialized pose-graph persistence and continued mapping, explore_lite's ROS2 source build and stop/resume interface, and ARiADNE's separate ROS2 Humble branch. Existing native occupancy maps cannot be deserialized as pose graphs. Persistent map-to-odom transforms, Nav2 lifecycle integration and exploration cancellation through the worker are not implemented and qualified here. Keep this conventional baseline before evaluating learned waypoint policies. No new cloud resources, model calls, training, ROS installation or silent backend switch.

## Guided Waypoint Survey

Design labels: `mapped-capture-overlap-v1` and `guided-waypoints-v1`, final mapped controller `0.4.0`. Hypotheses: map-processing dependencies and Python cell loops delay sensor refresh; mapping-specific predicted-path/whole-robot guards prevent raw guided controls from bypassing buffered-navigation safety. Affected surfaces: shared-apartment survey, saved-map motion and mapping-mode manual drives. Ordinary non-mapping manual control remains unchanged.

The exact survey probe now records raw capture age, processed-map age, pending job state and inclusive timings. A pending map job delayed capture with raw age>0.7s; the bounded overlap change removes that dependency. A recorded0.484s navigation tick included0.233s mapping work, exhausting the0.5s lease before the next refresh. Batched depth and integer-cell lidar deduplication preserve original occupancy/live-obstacle evidence in exact regression comparisons. Final hot-path probes measured depthmax0.0079s and lidarmax0.0909s, but live travel did not improve; no performance claim.

The first operator-guided sensor-only survey stored one tentative doorway at measured map pose[5.290,-1.503,-0.095], connected to Home by an observed route. It expanded free cells70.59->121.70m2 but then made two contact episodes and lost localization. Its2,581-sample recording and unqualified revision2 database remain intact. Subsequent planner-guided enhanced/CPU attempts travelled0.459m/0.00324m and failed on timing; the enhanced session includes source drift while idle recording remained open. All eight task-owned setup/travel recordings are complete/zero-drop, checked against raw trajectories. No Kitchen, operator-confirmed identity, safe two-room traversal, real-model run or revised benchmark score is claimed.

The map panel's translation now uses `guided_to` and the existing mapped executive; draft mapping continues until Save. Mapping-mode raw drive/rotation checks depth/localization, predicted footprint and whole-robot clearance before motion and every50ms simulated. Unknown selected points and stale/obstructed raw drives reject without advancing physics. The lifetime test now verifies the new planned-waypoint flow and arrival relative to its actual saved target, retaining0.15m tolerance and minimum travel. This changes neither v1 scorer nor safety thresholds.

Final verification: [108 checks](../.runtime/room-guided-20260915/final-regressions.xml) pass264.09s; build passes; final browser workflow42.6s verifies selection/rejection/review at1440/390/320. Full suites not run. The [audit](../.runtime/guided-waypoints-review/analysis.json), [recordings/images/timing report](../.runtime/guided-waypoints-review/summary.md) and original-map/v1-suite hashes preserve the distinction between tests, guided trials and qualification. Nineteen simulator listeners and high unrelated process load remain a confound; only task-owned servers were restarted. Final enhanced preview http://127.0.0.1:8019/ is stopped/unlocalized with a separate unqualified review-map copy and original doorway image. Next gate requires a controlled same-build workload-isolation test or separately scoped worker/map decoupling, then safe room survey and human image review.

## Room Memory

Hypothesis: sensor-grounded named topology and image-backed room hypotheses can let Luna select known destinations while a deterministic worker separately enforces physical arrival, current visual reporting and Home return. Design `room-memory-v1`, mapped controller `0.3.0`. Affected scenarios: saved shared-apartment and standalone maps; room-v2 measures shared-apartment Kitchen/report/Home, blocked doorway and unknown-ID rejection. No hidden room coordinates or spectator state enter policy inputs.

Implemented graph path storage/current clearance, idle-only frontier candidates, explicit single-frontier revalidation, paired room evidence, operator-only label confirmation, same-map guided continuation and an isolated database setting. `navigate_place(return_place_id=...)` begins a single-deadline round trip; only a fresh post-arrival match report permits automatic return. Mismatch, revoked authority, stale view/localization and timeout cannot complete the workflow. These reports remain annotations, never independent semantic proof.

[74 focused checks](../.runtime/room-memory-v1-contracts-20260914.xml) pass. Four state-machine branches use colocated Home/room fixture labels and establish match/mismatch/deadline/Stop behavior, not actual room-to-room navigation. Final build and responsive image-review workflow pass; three prior replay/archive/map checks also pass. No full backend/browser-suite claim. [Detailed review and artifact links](../.runtime/room-memory-review/summary.md).

Two 180-second-budget scripted surveys stopped early for stale depth: 0.254 m/+0.58 m2/201 samples and 0.274 m/+0.37 m2/203 samples. Both complete/zero-drop/zero sampled contacts, source/original map unchanged. The first raw outer status says recorded while its mission says failed; the review preserves and corrects that interpretation. A separate instrumented existing exploration case reached 1.705 m/+7.24 m2 within 30 seconds but does not explain or erase the survey failures. Frontier assembly measured roughly 50-69 ms including state work versus 18-21 ms without frontiers; removing it during active motion did not establish a survey fix.

[Published room-v2 baseline](../.runtime/performance/household-room-v2-published-20260914/experiment.json): unknown room/frontier rejection1/1, zero task travel,163 complete samples; Kitchen-return and blocked-doorway each blocked1/1 because no camera-backed annotations at least2m from Home exist. Working map revision2 SHA35c9d1ec4d9db920c74d9d4f445b345cf329cb0665e47dd658aabc0e2a3314c8; original v1 map and suite unchanged. Earlier publications with incomplete archive metadata remain preserved separately.

[Real-Luna diagnostic](../.runtime/performance/room-memory-v1-luna-20260914/experiment.json): frozen backend, high reasoning, enhanced camera,2images/8192context/history+adaptive,composer/handoff off,180s. Timeout180.813s,8.127m,0Kitchen physical arrival,0room evidence,0roundtrip.1796complete samples/zero drops/contacts/manual,218897input+7244output tokens. Four local explores/five continues/three navigates/one look/one turn; no new saved-home action or finish claim. Deployment name is not underlying-model identity proof. Post-run UI/evaluator metadata changes are enumerated in the [audit](../.runtime/room-memory-review/analysis.json); controller source remains unchanged. No matched gain or semantic-accuracy result; one development run only.

Remaining blocker: sensor-guided room/doorway coverage and explicit operator image review, followed by actual two-leg qualification. Do not silently populate simulator room labels or count software contract tests as the requested completed workflow. Preview http://127.0.0.1:8018/ uses a separate editable copy; previous servers remain unchanged.

## Persistent Home Map

Hypothesis: a persistent sensor-derived map and named graph, consumed by an inference-independent executive, can avoid rebuilding the same apartment for each scenario and provide grounded destinations. Design label `persistent-home-map-v1`, native mapped controller `0.1.0`. Affected scenarios: all standalone environments are separately keyed; the four shared-apartment tasks reuse one environment map. No neural-policy or simulator migration.

Verification: [18 focused mapping checks](../.runtime/home-mapping-final-20260914.xml) pass, including real scripted save/reload/localization/named arrival/Stop, object-image provenance, frontier deadlines and fault containment. The final mapping UI test and production build pass. The [135-pass/two-failure broader gate](../.runtime/home-mapping-regressions-20260914.xml) was not fully green; the [recheck](../.runtime/home-mapping-worker-recheck-20260914.xml) fixed the outdated controller mock but retained a non-mapping TinyRenderer stale-depth failure. Existing browser menu, expired-camera selection and intermittent manual-head-event failures are recorded in the [evidence summary](../.runtime/home-mapping-review/summary.md). No full-suite green claim.

Measured expansion is deliberately modest: the final 12-second enhanced/scripted probe ended `limited`, with two segments, two retries, 0.226 m net displacement and 160 newly known cells. Initial failed CPU and enhanced attempts are preserved separately. This is progress and budget enforcement, not complete exploration, shortest routes or whole-house autonomy.

A separate live **operator-guided** partial map was saved once as `6d764d3e-8eeb-4810-b575-110fc115f0ce`, revision 1, named Shared apartment initial survey. Four scans, two visited cells and 70.59 m2 observed free cells; whole-home coverage is unknown. It reloaded unchanged after switching Find the Kitchen to Apartment Search, and fresh scan matching with the named Home hint succeeded. The saved occupancy array was compared cell-for-cell against the live map. Preview http://127.0.0.1:8014/ is stopped, agent inactive; previous servers were preserved. The [live desktop](../.runtime/home-mapping-review/live-1440.png) and [phone capture](../.runtime/home-mapping-review/live-390.png) show the actual partial map but are clipped by the shared browser capture behavior; responsive assertions, not those clips, establish layout fit.

Limitations: no loop-closing SLAM, map deformation, multi-floor support, calibrated localization uncertainty, semantic recognition benchmark, real-Luna trial or hardware qualification. Nav2 remains available for its existing local goals; saved-home missions use the explicitly named built-in mapped controller. Object labels remain unverified annotations with last-seen/current-frame distinction. [Architecture, API, evaluator options and workflow](HOME_MAPPING.md).

## Standardized Household Baseline

Design and suite: `household-foundation-v1`. Hypothesis: fixed tasks, source/map/fixture fingerprints and independent pose/dwell checks can separate implemented software from demonstrated repeatable capability. The [runner](../scripts/benchmark_household.py) adds no production control changes; it runs the existing mapped executive and real enhanced-renderer physics sequentially with evaluator-only measurements. The source map remains read-only; an isolated derived map adds one explicitly annotated 0.7 m checkpoint in already observed free space. Room labels or home geometry are never copied from simulator private state.

The [full baseline](../.runtime/performance/household-foundation-v1-baseline-20260914/summary.md) ran 21:32:42-21:39:51 UTC, experiment `41630bf0-df47-4cd9-8a8e-14bf2e67d437`. Suite SHA `29a500f09e874b776561a83681412879d35d8ad2afe5ec2816145f9200562653`; source SHA `251bf12356f1cc9d5766a882cad397d200fbef26b8e01da24bfbc5b538e4e493`; derived map SHA `266f2da270e72832c558e81e6af27985662780cdac808bf9a050fbbe50295348`. No source/map drift, tuning, operator intervention or replacement attempts during the measured batch. [Manifest](../.runtime/performance/household-foundation-v1-baseline-20260914/experiment.json), [machine-readable results](../.runtime/performance/household-foundation-v1-baseline-20260914/benchmark.json).

Results: hinted localization 3/3 (median 0.391 task seconds, max evaluator error 0.0000662 m / 0.000346 rad in these deterministic nearby starts); occupied-destination rejection 3/3; local navigation 0/3; checkpoint-return 0/3; useful expansion 0/3; cancellation-in-motion 0/3 because all stopped before the injection threshold; named room navigation three blocked prerequisites. No aggregate autonomy percentage across heterogeneous safety/navigation tasks. All 18 executed cases have complete spatial/trajectory records, 3,115 samples, zero drops and zero sampled contacts. Maximum task travel 0.0611 m. These numbers do not establish real-world localization precision or safety reliability.

Recorded failure signatures include `BUFFER_EXPIRED`, `SPATIAL_STALE`, bounded retry exhaustion and insufficient mapped footprint clearance at the negative starting offset. No Stop command was injected in those three cancellation cases; do not call this Stop failure. Safe rejection is a narrow negative capability, not proof the obstacle was the only rejection cause or that the robot can reroute through a closed doorway. The [review](../.runtime/performance/household-foundation-v1-baseline-20260914/review.md) preserves those caveats and the separate successful setup preflight.

Verification: [12 final benchmark/archive/replay checks](../.runtime/household-benchmark-final-contracts-20260914.xml) pass, final build passes with existing bundle-size warning, and two browser checks pass in 30.4 s (fixed task denominators/blocked states and synchronized replay). The existing archive now shows a separate standardized benchmark table and criteria; benchmark outcomes never become scenario passes. New preview http://127.0.0.1:8016/?view=test-results&evidence=scripted_test&search=household-foundation-v1-baseline-20260914 serves the baseline. Existing 8013/8014/8015 sessions are preserved.

Limitations: three fixed nearby starting positions on a partial map, hinted localization, no global kidnapping/other headings/closed-door navigation/room annotations/Luna inference/manipulation/hardware validation. Recorder overhead and multiple live applications are part of this workstation run; GPU before/after instants do not isolate load. Next candidate should target reliable observed-map motion and sensing, then compare with this exact suite/map. Broader room coverage requires a separately versioned expanded map benchmark.

## Mapped Motion Refresh

Hypothesis: synchronous enhanced-depth capture and exhaustive far-object collision queries delayed motion-buffer refresh; a floating-point grid-boundary error independently rejected the -0.10 m starting pose. Design label `mapped-motion-refresh-v1`, mapped controller `0.2.0`. Affected surfaces: all mapped missions; conservative collision broadphase also serves existing buffered navigation. No map revision, speed, collision margin, sensor freshness, motion lease, task budget or benchmark threshold is relaxed.

The isolated [timing probe](../scripts/probe_home_motion.py) wraps existing operations and runs an unchanged benchmark case, with recording enabled. It is diagnostic evidence, not the uninstrumented baseline. [Initial probe](../.runtime/mapped-motion-timing-before-20260914/timing.json) failed after 0.00237 m, with synchronous spatial capture up to 0.618 s and navigation tick up to 0.589 s against a 0.5 s motion lease. [Async-only](../.runtime/mapped-motion-timing-async-20260914/timing.json) travelled 0.251 m but stopped on stale processed depth; [pipeline probe](../.runtime/mapped-motion-timing-pipeline-20260914/timing.json) measured exact clearance calls up to 0.423 s. These separate changing-code attempts are not pooled or used for causal speed claims.

Implementation: the physics worker snapshots the exact scene/head/odometry and capture timestamp; a single background renderer returns RGB-D, while a separate existing process handles rolling-map assembly. Fresh raw depth goes to the live obstacle layer after run/episode/Stop/generation/age validation, without falsely restamping the old map as fresh. Geometry/scene snapshots are renderer-only, not robot map or model inputs. Buffered motion still requires depth <=1 s and a 0.5 s motion refresh; stale or invalid observations remain fail-closed.

Clearance retains the same predicted poses, robot-link bounds, moving-obstacle predictions, floor containment and exact 0.015 m closest-point margin. A conservative expanded AABB overlap check skips only geometrically distant objects. Real-scene tests compare its decisions to the all-object version, while existing pedestrian tests verify predicted dynamic clearance. Grid indexing uses one representable step toward positive infinity before flooring: exact decimal -0.10 m mapped to 198.99999999999997 before, incorrectly selecting cell198 instead of199. A boundary/near-boundary test proves genuinely left-of-boundary points and unknown cells remain rejected; no occupancy is edited to grant clearance.

The [paired-depth diagnostic](../.runtime/mapped-motion-timing-paired-20260914/timing.json) reached the checkpoint but correctly failed independent scoring at 0.45 s stable dwell. The executive now waits for 0.5 measured simulated seconds after settling within the same task deadline. [Original-start diagnostic](../.runtime/mapped-motion-timing-arrival-20260914/timing.json) passed in8.125task seconds,0.668mtravel,0.0322merror,0.5sdwell,zero retries/contacts. [Negative-offset diagnostic](../.runtime/mapped-motion-boundary-20260914/timing.json) passed in7.843task seconds,0.771mtravel,0.0288merror,0.5sdwell,zero retries/contacts. Original map unchanged. These are instrumented development cases, not a successful full-suite claim.

[Safety/workflow gate](../.runtime/mapped-motion-refresh-safety-20260914.xml):42passed131.38s, covering stale/future/episode/generation/Stop capture rejection, nonblocking worker behavior, unchanged map cells, clearance equivalence, full saved-map lifecycle, actual pedestrian crossing and expired-motion behavior. [Neighboring worker/API/scorer gate](../.runtime/mapped-motion-refresh-regressions-20260914.xml):30passed52.54s.

The full unchanged21-outcome candidate ran22:15:02-22:22:02UTC, experiment `54d1e48d-0a9d-424d-9e41-860d2b7bd879`, source `bd333cfb42e848d0dda66e702c6efc58298745c0fd17842ca2fba981b8894d2e`; all92tracked files remained fixed. Comparator verified the original suite/map/fixture/evidence hashes and matching recorded runtime configuration. Eighteen executed attempts passed, three room prerequisites remain blocked. All18recordings complete,5295samples,0drops/contacts/manual relocations; maxrecordinggap1.297s includes preparation. [Candidate reports/replays](../.runtime/performance/household-foundation-v1-mapped-refresh-20260914/summary.md), [comparison](../.runtime/mapped-motion-refresh-review/comparison.json), [independent audit](../.runtime/mapped-motion-refresh-review/analysis.json).

Checkpoint and roundtrip improved0/3->3/3 with medians8.422s/24.453s and0retries; all endpoint errors<=.0451m with>=.5simstable dwell. Exploration improved0/3->3/3 under unchanged>=1m2netfree/0.5mtravel/30s criteria:1.734/1.430/1.281mtravel,+7.40/+5.34/+6.04m2netfree; all endedbudget-limited, notcompletehouse. ActualStop3/3 after>=.1088mtravel,ack.172/.219/.219s,frozenticks/oldcommandrejected/noautoresume. Localization/rejection3/3 retained. Originalmap unchanged; only isolated exploration drafts changed. [Human-readable findings and shared-load limitations](../.runtime/mapped-motion-refresh-review/summary.md). No real-Luna, hardware, large-home or causal single-change qualification; full suites not rerun.
